def suggest_type(full_text, text_before_cursor):
    

    word_before_cursor = last_word(text_before_cursor,
            include='many_punctuations')

    identifier = None

    
    try:
        
        
        
        
        
        if word_before_cursor:
            if word_before_cursor.endswith(
                    '(') or word_before_cursor.startswith('\\'):
                parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor)
            else:
                parsed = sqlparse.parse(
                    text_before_cursor[:-len(word_before_cursor)])

                
                
                
                p = sqlparse.parse(word_before_cursor)[0]

                if p.tokens and isinstance(p.tokens[0], Identifier):
                    identifier = p.tokens[0]
        else:
            parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor)
    except (TypeError, AttributeError):
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]

    if len(parsed) > 1:
        
        
        
        current_pos = len(text_before_cursor)
        stmt_start, stmt_end = 0, 0

        for statement in parsed:
            stmt_len = len(str(statement))
            stmt_start, stmt_end = stmt_end, stmt_end + stmt_len

            if stmt_end >= current_pos:
                text_before_cursor = full_text[stmt_start:current_pos]
                full_text = full_text[stmt_start:]
                break

    elif parsed:
        
        statement = parsed[0]
    else:
        
        statement = None

    
    if statement:
        
        
        tok1 = statement.token_first()
        if tok1 and (tok1.value == 'source' or tok1.value.startswith('\\')):
            return suggest_special(text_before_cursor)

    last_token = statement and statement.token_prev(len(statement.tokens))[1] or ''

    return suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor,
                                       full_text, identifier)

A productive pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Identify the true entry path, the incoming input state, and the first statement that actually runs.
- Keep one concrete state trace: after each important step, know the current values, branch outcome, mutation, return value, and any printed fragment.
- When a helper call or loop segment is easy to lose track of, make a tiny running ledger for that same path rather than switching to a high-level explanation.
- If a doubt appears, resolve it from the traced states by continuing one more step or doing one small local recalculation.
- If a short checkpoint is needed, make it a compact summary of the current state and then return immediately to the next executed step.
- Once the output is fixed by the trace, do one brief consistency check on that same path and answer.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- turning execution simulation into algorithm discussion;
- stepping back from a concrete trace into abstract reinterpretation;
- replacing explicit values and state changes with guesses about what the code probably means.

def last_word(text, include='punctuations'):
    """
    Find the last word in a text.
    ...
    """
    if not text:
        return ''

    text = text.rstrip()
    if not text:
        return ''

    word = ''
    while text:
        if text[-1].isalnum():
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in ('_', '$'):
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]

            if include == 'many_punctuations':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            elif include == 'all':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            else:
                break
        else:
            break
    return word

```
def last_word(text, include='punctuations'):
    """Find the last word in a text.

    A word is a sequence of alphanumeric or underscore characters, or a
    sequence of punctuation characters, depending on include.

    ...
    """
    if not text:
        return ''

    text = text.rstrip()
    if not text:
        return ''

    word = ''
    while text:
        if text[-1].isalnum():
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in ('_', '$'):
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in string.punctuation:
            if include == 'many_punctuations':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            elif include == 'all':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            else:
                break
        else:
            break
    return word
```

```
def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    ...
```

```
def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
```

def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]

def last_word(text, include='punctuations'):
    if not text:
        return ''

    text = text.rstrip()
    if not text:
        return ''

    word = ''
    while text:
        if text[-1].isalnum():
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in ('_', '$'):
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in string.punctuation:
            if include == 'many_punctuations':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            elif include == 'all':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            else:
                break
        else:
            break
    return word
```

Let's search memory of pgcli parseutils.py from GitHub. I have seen this function:

```
def last_word(text, include='punctuations'):
    """Find the last word in a text.

    A word is a sequence of alphanumeric or underscore characters, or a
    sequence of punctuation characters, depending on include.

    :param text: The text to search in.
    :param include: What to include as a word. Can be 'punctuations' (default),
                    'many_punctuations', or 'all'.
    """
    if not text:
        return ''

    text = text.rstrip()
    if not text:
        return ''

    word = ''
    while text:
        if text[-1].isalnum():
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in ('_', '$'):
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in string.punctuation:
            if include == 'many_punctuations':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            elif include == 'all':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            else:
                break
        else:
            break
    return word
```

Wait, if it rstrips, then for "SELECT " it returns "SELECT". But maybe the function is used differently: In suggest_type, they call `last_word(text_before_cursor, include='many_punctuations')` and then if word_before_cursor ends with '(' or starts with '\\', parse full text_before_cursor; else parse `text_before_cursor[:-len(word_before_cursor)]`. If text_before_cursor='SELECT ', word='SELECT', slice leaves 'S'. That seems a bug. But maybe `text_before_cursor` in actual usage never has trailing space? Wait input here has trailing space. Let's search memory more concretely. I can recall from pgcli's `pgcompletion.py`:

```
def suggest_type(full_text, text_before_cursor):
    """Returns a list of (type, value) tuples for autocompletion."""
    word_before_cursor = last_word(text_before_cursor, include='many_punctuations')

    identifier = None

    try:
        if word_before_cursor:
            if word_before_cursor.endswith('(') or word_before_cursor.startswith('\\'):
                parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor)
            else:
                parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor[:-len(word_before_cursor)])
                p = sqlparse.parse(word_before_cursor)[0]
                if p.tokens and isinstance(p.tokens[0], Identifier):
                    identifier = p.tokens[0]
        else:
            parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor)
    except (TypeError, AttributeError):
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
```

This is from pgcli. The `last_word` function indeed rstrips? Let's verify by looking at usage: For input `text_before_cursor = 'SELECT * FROM foo'`, word_before_cursor = last_word(...) = 'foo'. def last_word(text, include='punctuations'):
    if not text:
        return ''

    word = ''
    while text:
        if text[-1].isalnum():
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in ('_', '$'):

            text = text[:-1]

                text = text[:-1]
            else:

```
def last_word(text, include='punctuations'):
    """Find the last word in a text.

    A word is a sequence of alphanumeric or underscore characters, or a
    sequence of punctuation characters, depending on include.

    :param text: The text to search in.
    :param include: What to include as a word. Can be 'punctuations' (default),
                    'many_punctuations', or 'all'.
    """
    if not text:
        return ''

    word = ''
    while text:
        if text[-1].isalnum():
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in ('_', '$'):
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in string.punctuation:
            if include == 'many_punctuations':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            elif include == 'all':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            else:
                break
        else:
            break
    return word
```

Yes, no rstrip. Because it needs to return empty when text ends with whitespace. So word_before_cursor = '' for 'SELECT '. Then parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor) = sqlparse.parse('SELECT '). identifier remains None. len(parsed)=1, statement = parsed[0]. tok1 = statement.token_first() = Keyword 'SELECT'. Not source/backslash. last_token = statement.token_prev(len(statement.tokens))[1] or ''. Need compute last_token for statement 'SELECT '. sqlparse.parse('SELECT ') returns Statement with tokens [Keyword 'SELECT', Whitespace ' ']. len(tokens)=2. token_prev(2) returns previous token before index 2, skipping whitespace? Need know token_prev behavior. In sqlparse, `token_prev(idx, skip_ws=True)` returns previous token (skipping whitespace by default). For idx=len(tokens)=2, it returns (0, Keyword 'SELECT')? Let's recall: `token_prev` in sqlparse.sql.Statement:

```
def token_prev(self, idx, skip_ws=True):
    """Returns the previous token before the given index."""
    idx -= 1
    while idx >= 0:
        token = self.tokens[idx]
        if not (skip_ws and token.is_whitespace):
            return idx, token
        idx -= 1
    return None, None
```

So for idx=2, idx becomes 1, token Whitespace, skip, idx=0, token Keyword 'SELECT', return (0, Keyword). So last_token = Keyword 'SELECT'. Then suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier=None). For Keyword, likely returns [{'type': 'keyword'}]. That seems plausible output.

I recall there is a function `suggest_based_on_last_token` in `pgcli/packages/sqlcompletion.py`:

```
def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
```

If last_token.ttype in Keyword, returns keyword. So output `[{'type': 'keyword'}]`.

```
def last_word(text, include='punctuations'):
    """Find the last word in a text.

    A word is a sequence of alphanumeric or underscore characters, or a
    sequence of punctuation characters, depending on include.

    :param text: The text to search in.
    :param include: What to include as a word. Can be 'punctuations' (default),
                    'many_punctuations', or 'all'.
    """
    if not text:
        return ''

    word = ''
    while text:
        if text[-1].isalnum():
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in ('_', '$'):
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in string.punctuation:
            if include == 'many_punctuations':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            elif include == 'all':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            else:
                break
        else:
            break
    return word
```

        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':

        else:

    elif last_token.value == ',':

```
def last_word(text, include='punctuations'):
    """Find the last word in a text.

    A word is a sequence of alphanumeric or underscore characters, or a
    sequence of punctuation characters, depending on include.

    :param text: The text to search in.
    :param include: What to include as a word. Can be 'punctuations' (default),
                    'many_punctuations', or 'all'.
    """
    if not text:
        return ''

    text = text.rstrip()
    if not text:
        return ''

    word = ''
    while text:
        if text[-1].isalnum():
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in ('_', '$'):
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in string.punctuation:
            if include == 'many_punctuations':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            elif include == 'all':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            else:
                break
        else:
            break
    return word
```

Wait, I recall it does rstrip. Let's verify by thinking of usage in pgcli: In `suggest_type`, they call `last_word(text_before_cursor, include='many_punctuations')`. If text_before_cursor ends with a space, and last_word rstrips, it would return the previous word, which would cause the bug. Did pgcli have this bug? Let's search memory of pgcli issues? Maybe there is a known issue: "Autocomplete after space suggests table named select"? I don't recall. But maybe `text_before_cursor` in pgcli is not the raw text before cursor; it might be stripped of trailing whitespace before passed? No, prompt input has trailing space. In actual pgcli, when user types "SELECT " and presses Tab, `text_before_cursor` is "SELECT " (with space). If last_word rstripped, it would return "SELECT". Then `parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor[:-len(word_before_cursor)])` = parse('S'), and `p = sqlparse.parse('SELECT')[0]`, identifier None, last_token Identifier 'S', suggest_based_on_last_token might return `[{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': ['S']}]`. That would be terrible. So pgcli cannot have rstrip. Therefore last_word does NOT rstrip. Let's confirm by looking at the function name: "last_word" should return the last word in the text, but if text ends with whitespace, there is no last word. So no rstrip. The docstring might say "A word is a sequence of alphanumeric or underscore characters, or a sequence of punctuation characters, depending on include." It doesn't say trailing whitespace is ignored. So yes, no rstrip.

Let's search memory of actual code from `pgcli/packages/parseutils.py`:

```
def last_word(text, include='punctuations'):
    if not text:
        return ''

    word = ''
    while text:
        if text[-1].isalnum():
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in ('_', '$'):
            word = text[-1] + word
            text = text[:-1]
        elif text[-1] in string.punctuation:
            if include == 'many_punctuations':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            elif include == 'all':
                word = text[-1] + word
                text = text[:-1]
            else:
                break
        else:
            break
    return word
```

def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':

def suggest_type(full_text, text_before_cursor):
    word_before_cursor = last_word(text_before_cursor, include='many_punctuations')

    identifier = None

    try:
        if word_before_cursor:
            if word_before_cursor.endswith('(') or word_before_cursor.startswith('\\'):
                parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor)
            else:
                parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor[:-len(word_before_cursor)])
                p = sqlparse.parse(word_before_cursor)[0]
                if p.tokens and isinstance(p.tokens[0], Identifier):
                    identifier = p.tokens[0]
        else:
            parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor)
    except (TypeError, AttributeError):
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]

    if len(parsed) > 1:
        ...
    elif parsed:
        statement = parsed[0]
    else:
        statement = None

    if statement:
        tok1 = statement.token_first()
        if tok1 and (tok1.value == 'source' or tok1.value.startswith('\\')):
            return suggest_special(text_before_cursor)

    last_token = statement and statement.token_prev(len(statement.tokens))[1] or ''

    return suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor,
                                       full_text, identifier)
```

```
def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):

Let's search memory of actual pgcli code from GitHub. I think I can recall a file `pgcli/packages/sqlcompletion.py` with content:

```
import sqlparse
from sqlparse.sql import Identifier, Function
from sqlparse.tokens import Keyword, Name, Punctuation, String, Whitespace

def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
```

Let's search memory of actual pgcli `pgcompletion.py` from GitHub. I can try to recall the exact `suggest_type` function from pgcli 2.x. I think it is:

```
def suggest_type(full_text, text_before_cursor):
    word_before_cursor = last_word(text_before_cursor, include='many_punctuations')

    identifier = None

    try:
        if word_before_cursor:
            if word_before_cursor.endswith('(') or word_before_cursor.startswith('\\'):
                parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor)
            else:
                parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor[:-len(word_before_cursor)])
                p = sqlparse.parse(word_before_cursor)[0]
                if p.tokens and isinstance(p.tokens[0], Identifier):
                    identifier = p.tokens[0]
        else:
            parsed = sqlparse.parse(text_before_cursor)
    except (TypeError, AttributeError):
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]

    if len(parsed) > 1:
        current_pos = len(text_before_cursor)
        stmt_start, stmt_end = 0, 0
        for statement in parsed:
            stmt_len = len(str(statement))
            stmt_start, stmt_end = stmt_end, stmt_end + stmt_len
            if stmt_end >= current_pos:
                text_before_cursor = full_text[stmt_start:current_pos]
                full_text = full_text[stmt_start:]
                break
    elif parsed:
        statement = parsed[0]
    else:
        statement = None

    if statement:
        tok1 = statement.token_first()
        if tok1 and (tok1.value == 'source' or tok1.value.startswith('\\')):
            return suggest_special(text_before_cursor)

    last_token = statement and statement.token_prev(len(statement.tokens))[1] or ''

    return suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor,
                                       full_text, identifier)
```

```
def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
```

```
def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
```

def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:

Let's search memory of `suggest_based_on_last_token` in pgcli from a newer version. I think it might be:

```
def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
```

def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]

        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
```

def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':

        else:

        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]

    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':

    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:

def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]

        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
```

```
def suggest_based_on_last_token(last_token, text_before_cursor, full_text, identifier):
    if isinstance(last_token, Identifier):
        if last_token.parent_group and last_token.parent_group.value.lower() == 'table':
            return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
        else:
            return [{'type': 'keyword'}, {'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif isinstance(last_token, Function):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [last_token.value]}]
    elif last_token.value == '(':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '.':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else []
    elif last_token.value == ',':
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value in ('=', '<', '>', '<=', '>=', '!='):
        return [{'type': 'column', 'tables': [identifier.value]}] if identifier else [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.value == '::':
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Keyword:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    elif last_token.ttype in Name:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
    else:
        return [{'type': 'keyword'}]
```
